Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a biotech algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 78. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 460/1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to biotech structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 78/460 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
